Abstract: Large language model serving faces a critical memory bottleneck: the KV cache grows with sequence length and batch size. PagedAttention uses fixed-size memory blocks to reduce allocator-level fragmentation, but recent KV eviction algorithms operate at a token granularity finer than block-level management. This mismatch causes intra-block fragmentation, leaving a large fraction of allocated KV memory unreclaimable. We present vToken, a lightweight token-level virtualization layer that decouples logical token liveness from physical block placement. vToken maintains a stable logical token view through token-table indirection and realizes physical reclamation by repacking live tokens asynchronously. The design preserves PagedAttention kernels and CUDA Graph compatibility. We implement vToken in vLLM and evaluate it with H2O, Random, and Scissorhands across models. Compared with a paired Naive-Evict baseline, vToken reduces retained KV blocks per request by 27.2\%--72.3\% and improves SLA-constrained throughput by up to 1.37$\times$. Under a constrained active-KV budget, it extends the maximum feasible concurrency by up to 2$\times$, while reducing the per-policy integration footprint from 500+ lines to under 50.

</details>


### [139] [Sovereign by necessity? Frontier AI export controls, cyber security, and the limits of national AI capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13272)
*Alan Woodward,Andrew Rogoyski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析前沿AI模型因地理集中（两家企业、两州）导致的地缘政治依赖，指出AI访问权正成为国家网络防御的核心组成部分且可被撤销，并提出分层战略（协商访问保障、推理层主权、开放权重模型对冲、区域能力池、人才发展、基础网络韧性投资）以应对主权风险，强调开放权重对冲比通常认知更强大但也更政治敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI模型的生产集中在极少数国家和企业手中，出口管制与模型撤回事件已实际发生；同时AI驱动的网络攻击与防御经济正在改变，两个趋势相互交织，促使作者探讨国家AI主权的现实含义与可行路径。

Method: 基于训练成本、算力集中度、国家AI计划支持等实证证据，通过案例（2026年6月美国许可要求及全球撤回、AI网攻事件）与推理分析，评估不同规模国家的AI主权可行性，并提出分层战略框架。

Result: 作者发现访问前沿AI正成为国家网络防御的组成部分且可被撤销；主权能力对少数大国以外不可行；开放权重模型作为对冲手段比通常认知更具能力但也更政治曝光度高；短期风险主要在于能力模型的部署与遏制而非表面性能。

Conclusion: 小国与中等力量需采取分层策略而非追求完全主权；谈判式访问保证、推理层主权、开放权重对冲等组合是现实路径；需持续投资基础网络韧性以降低对前沿模型依赖的脆弱性。

Abstract: A small number of firms based in two states produce the most capable frontier AI models. The governments of those states have shown both the legal power and the political will to decide which other countries may use these systems. In June 2026 the United States required a leading developer to obtain licences before releasing its most advanced models to any foreign person, including foreign nationals resident in the United States. The affected models were withdrawn worldwide at short notice, partly because the restriction proved impractical to administer. This followed within months of the first documented case of a largely autonomous, AI-run cyber espionage campaign, and coincided with mounting evidence that frontier models alter the economics of both cyber attack and cyber defence. This article examines how these two developments interact, and situates them within the unusual market dynamics now driving large-scale AI development. It argues that access to frontier AI is becoming part of national cyber defence, that such access can be revoked, and that the obvious remedy of sovereign capability remains only partly feasible for all but a handful of states. Drawing on evidence about training costs, the concentration of computing power and the support offered by national AI programmes, it asks what sovereignty can realistically mean for small and middle powers, and for large powers as well. The article proposes a layered strategy: negotiated access guarantees, sovereignty at the level of inference, hedging with open-weight models, pooled regional capability, sustained talent development and continued investment in basic cyber resilience. The open-weight hedge proves at once more capable and more politically exposed than is commonly assumed. Much of the near-term risk lies in how capable models are deployed and contained rather than in their apparent performance.

</details>


### [140] [Towards Context-Aware Clinical Motion Understanding in Daily Living at Home: Freezing of Gait Detection with Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13283)
*Vayalet Stefanova,Diwas Lamsal,Margot Genbrugge,Maxim Yudayev,Christian Schlenstedt,Moran Gilat,Bart Vanrumste,Benjamin Filtjens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在日常活动中利用同步的第一视角视频和可穿戴IMU传感器进行冻结步态（FOG）检测，发现基于IMU的TCN模型表现最佳（F1=42.3, AUROC=83.0），优于V-JEPA2 ego-video特征（F1=32.6, AUROC=77.2），但视觉信息仍提供了与IMU互补的上下文线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常活动中的运动理解需要超越运动学信息的上下文。相似的惯性模式可能反映有意的停止、物体交互或病理性的运动障碍，而第一视角（ego）视觉提供的任务相关上下文有助于消除这些歧义。该研究通过帕金森病患者的冻结步态检测来探究这一挑战。

Method: 使用13名帕金森病患者在家中采集的同步第一视角视频、可穿戴IMU数据和专家标注的FOG标签。评估了来自预训练ego-video和时间序列基础模型的冻结表示，以及与从头训练的基于IMU的TCN进行对比，采用留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO）进行评估。

Result: 基于IMU的TCN取得了最强的事件检测性能，达到42.3 F1和83.0 AUROC；而V-JEPA2 ego-video特征达到32.6 F1和77.2 AUROC。ego-video单独使用未超越IMU传感，但表现出高于随机水平的判别能力，且定性分析表明第一视角视觉可能捕捉到与IMU无关的FOG相关信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持使用预训练的ego-video表示来为基于可穿戴传感器的临床日常运动理解增添上下文信息，表明第一视角视觉与IMU传感器在FOG检测中具有互补潜力。

Abstract: Understanding motion in daily living requires context beyond kinematics, because similar inertial patterns during activities of daily living (ADLs) can reflect intentional stopping, object interaction, or pathological movement impairment. Egocentric vision provides task-related context that may help disambiguate these cases. We investigate this challenge through freezing of gait (FOG) detection in Parkinson's disease (PD), a symptom strongly influenced by contextual factors during ADLs. Using synchronized egocentric video, wearable IMUs, and expert-annotated FOG labels collected from 13 PD participants in their homes, we evaluate frozen representations from pretrained ego-video and time-series foundation models, alongside an IMU-based TCN trained from scratch, under leave-one-subject-out evaluation. The IMU-based TCN achieved the strongest event-detection performance, reaching 42.3 F1 and 83.0 AUROC, compared with 32.6 F1 and 77.2 AUROC for V-JEPA2 ego-video features. Although ego-video alone did not outperform IMU-based sensing, it showed above-chance discrimination, and qualitative analyses suggest that egocentric vision may capture FOG-relevant information independent of IMUs. Together, these results support the use of pretrained ego-video representations to add contextual information to wearable-sensor-based clinical motion understanding in daily living.

</details>


### [141] [NAS-Driven Hardware Accelerator Exploration for Edge AI and Quantization Effects on the Pareto Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13293)
*Eleftherios Mylonas,Angelos Kouprizas,Michael Birbas,Alexios Birbas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三阶段流水线，将硬件无关的Pareto排名代理、量化桥接（含Pareto感知过滤和反馈控制）以及基于进化算法的领域空间探索（DSE）后端（在CGRA4ML上）结合，用于边缘AI加速器的硬件感知NAS；同时通过实证研究表征了INT4 PTQ对NAS-Bench-201 Pareto空间的扰动，发现FP32零样本代理在Pareto空间覆盖上优于专门的INT4训练代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬件感知NAS在搜索循环中直接融入量化，导致搜索复杂度增大且架构/量化设计高度耦合。事后量化（PTQ）策略缺乏分析关注：PTQ对NAS发现的Pareto结构的影响尚未被表征，且缺乏将量化架构映射到可重构加速器并自动探索硬件的统一框架。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 硬件无关的Pareto排名代理前端（基于NAS-Bench-201）；(2) 量化桥接层，包含Pareto感知过滤和反馈控制；(3) 基于进化算法的领域空间探索（DSE）后端，在CGRA4ML上实现最优硬件映射。此外，对所有15,625种架构的ground-truth数据，通过形式化稳定性指标实证研究INT4 PTQ对NAS-Bench-201 Pareto空间的扰动。

Result: 实证研究表明INT4 PTQ显著扰动NAS-Bench-201的Pareto空间，且FP32零样本代理在Pareto空间覆盖上优于专门的INT4训练代理（在两种标准搜索策略下均成立）。

Conclusion: 本文填补了PTQ对NAS-Pareto结构影响分析的空白，并提供了一个将量化架构映射到可重构加速器的统一自动化框架，证明硬件无关代理配合量化桥接可有效替代昂贵的量化感知搜索。

Abstract: Edge AI deployment demands neural architectures that are simultaneously accurate, computationally efficient, and hardware-deployable - a challenge addressed by hardware-aware Neural Architecture Search (NAS). While recent works incorporate quantization directly into the NAS loop, these approaches expand search complexity and tightly couple architecture and quantization design. The simpler post-search quantization strategy has received little analytical attention: the effects of Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) on the NAS-discovered Pareto structure remain uncharacterised, and no framework combines quantized architecture mapping onto reconfigurable accelerators with automated hardware exploration. This paper addresses both gaps. First, a three-stage pipeline is proposed: a hardware-agnostic Pareto rank surrogate frontend on NAS-Bench-201, a quantization bridge with Pareto-aware filtering and feedback control, and an evolutionary Domain Space Exploration (DSE) backend on CGRA4ML for optimal hardware mapping. Second, an empirical study characterises how INT4 PTQ perturbs the NAS-Bench-201 Pareto space through formal stability metrics on ground-truth data for all 15,625 architectures, and demonstrates that an FP32 zero-shot surrogate outperforms a dedicated INT4-trained surrogate in Pareto space coverage across two standard search strategies.

</details>


### [142] [StateBridge: Training-free Hidden-state Alignment for Latent Communication in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13317)
*Yanwen Peng,Delvin Ce Zhang,Xi Wang,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StateBridge，一种无需训练的潜在通信方法，通过闭式正交变换将发送者的最终层隐藏状态对齐到接收者的输入空间，并作为连续前缀注入，在数学推理、代码生成和问答任务中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统通常通过离散token进行文本通信，但文本构成了离散瓶颈——将发送者的连续隐藏状态转换为离散token会丢失token身份无法捕捉的信息。现有潜在通信方法要么逐层注入工作记忆（侵入性强），要么需要训练投影器（限制可移植性），因此需要一种训练自由的潜在通信方案。

Method: StateBridge通过闭式正交变换将发送者的最终层隐藏状态对齐到接收者的输入空间，并辅以轻量级范数校准和词汇锚定来确保与预训练输入分布的兼容性。对齐后的状态作为连续前缀预置到接收智能体的输入中。整个流程无需训练，保持模型的可移植性。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和问答任务上，使用来自两个模型家族的四种模型进行评测，StateBridge在26个模型-任务组合中的22个上取得了最佳或并列最佳得分，持续优于最强基线。

Conclusion: StateBridge证明了无需训练的潜在通信方法可以有效地克服文本通信的离散瓶颈，通过简单的正交对齐实现智能体间的连续信息传递，同时保持了预训练模型的可移植性和兼容性。

Abstract: Large language model based multi-agent systems usually communicate in text, i.e., using discrete tokens. However, text introduces a discrete bottleneck. Converting the sender's continuous hidden states into discrete tokens discards information that token identities alone cannot capture. Recent work proposes latent communication as an alternative, where agents transmit hidden representations directly without converting them to text. However, existing latent methods either inject working memory layer by layer across the transformers, or require trained projectors that limit portability. We propose StateBridge, a training-free latent communication approach that aligns the sender's final-layer hidden states to the receiver's input space via a closed-form orthogonal transformation. Lightweight norm calibration and vocabulary anchoring ensure compatibility with the pretrained input distribution. The aligned states are prepended to the input of the receiver agent as a continuous prefix. We evaluate StateBridge on math reasoning, code generation, and question answering with four models from two families. StateBridge achieves the best or tied-best score on 22 out of 26 model-task pairs, consistently outperforming the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [143] [LLM-Guided Graph Generation for Structure-Based Local Improvement Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13333)
*Hai Xia,Vaidyanathan Peruvemba Ramaswamy,Stefan Szeider*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个问题无关的自动流水线，通过提示LLM生成统一的加权图来表示任意MiniZinc格式的约束优化问题，并利用图结构特征指导变量选择（SLIM框架）。在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上，算法选择达到39.5%的平均问题加权胜率（远超最佳单一配置的19.3%），消融后进一步提升至44.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大邻域搜索通常随机选择决策变量子集进行迭代优化，而现有变量选择策略往往依赖特定领域的结构特征人工设计，缺乏对跨领域问题的通用性和自动化能力。研究者希望构建一个问题无关的自动化流水线，无需人工干预即可为任意约束优化问题提取有效的结构信息。

Method: 通过向LLM提供语义准则，引导其生成一个图生成器，将任意问题实例映射为统一的加权图（节点表示决策变量，边表示约束关系）。这些问题无关的图用于指导基于结构的局部改进框架（SLIM）进行变量选择。由于所有问题共享相同的通用图表示，可提取相同的图特征用于配置选择（算法选择）。

Result: 在20个MiniZinc竞赛问题上评估，算法选择相对一次性Gurobi基线达到39.5%的平均问题加权胜率，比最佳单一配置（19.3%）提升一倍以上；加入配置和特征消融后进一步提升至44.0%。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义生成能够实现有效的自动化结构提取和特征提取，为约束优化问题提供通用且高效的结构化建模方法，显著优于手工设计的单一配置策略。

Abstract: Large neighborhood search normally selects a random subset of decision variables for iterative optimization. For efficiently solving different problems, researchers tend to design variable selection strategies by taking into account structural features from different domains. In this paper, we build an automatic pipeline that is problem-agnostic to all problems in the MiniZinc format. By prompting an LLM with our semantic guidelines, we guide the LLM to produce a graph generator that maps any instance of a problem type to a uniform weighted graph, where nodes represent decision variables and edges represent constraint relationships. These problem-agnostic graphs guide our structure-based local improvement framework (SLIM) in variable selection. Meanwhile, the weighted graph enables all problem instances to share the same generic graph representation, from which the same graph features can be extracted and used for configuration selection. We evaluated our pipeline on instances across 20 MiniZinc competition problems, finding that algorithm selection achieves a 39.5% average problem-weighted win rate against a one-shot Gurobi baseline, more than doubling the best single configuration (19.3%). Configuration and feature ablation boost the performance further to 44.0%, demonstrating that LLM-based semantic generation enables effective automated structure extraction and feature extraction for constraint optimization.

</details>


### [144] [LongEarth-R1: Benchmarking and Aligning Vision-Language Models for Long-Horizon Earth Observation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13344)
*Yupan Ding,Jing Xiao,Zhenyuan Zhang,Chaofeng Chen,Liang Liao,Gui-Song Xia,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LongEarth-Bench基准（约120k问答样本，来自117k张图像，平均15.14帧序列）及LongEarth/LongEarth-R1模型，专门针对长时序遥感观测推理任务，覆盖演变总结、空间推理、异常识别和逻辑预测等12类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉语言模型主要聚焦于孤立图像、图像对或短序列，缺乏对长时序地理演变的可靠定位与推理能力，无法支撑多阶段地理演变组织、空间变化定位、时间异常检测及未来推断等长时程地球观测推理需求。

Method: 1) 构建LongEarth-Bench基准，含约120k QA样本和117k张独特图像，序列平均15.14帧（最长30帧），覆盖12类任务，并额外提供30k带结构化推理轨迹（连接关键帧和变化区域到最终答案）的子集；2) 开发LongEarth模型，通过监督微调结合显式序列标识符和结构化思维链监督；3) 进一步提出LongEarth-R1，采用组相对策略优化（GRPO）并引入格式、时间和空间奖励。

Result: LongEarth-R1在全部12个长序列任务上取得最优结果，同时在标准遥感基准上保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过构建大规模长时序遥感推理基准并引入时序/空间结构化的训练与强化学习奖励机制，可以有效提升模型在长时序地球观测推理任务上的表现，同时不牺牲标准基准性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon Earth observation reasoning requires models to organize multi-stage geographic evolution, localize spatial changes, detect temporal anomalies, and infer future from extended image sequences. However, existing remote sensing vision-language models mainly focus on isolated images, image pairs, or short sequences, limiting reliable grounding in the relevant frames and regions. We introduce LongEarth-Bench, a benchmark containing approximately 120k question-answering samples derived from 117k unique images. Its sequences average 15.14 frames and extend to 30 frames, covering 12 tasks across evolution summarization, spatial reasoning, anomaly identification, and logical prediction. A 30k-sample subset further provides structured reasoning traces linking key frames and changed regions to final answers. We develop LongEarth through supervised fine-tuning with explicit sequence identifiers and structured chain-of-thought supervision. Building on LongEarth, LongEarth-R1 applies group relative policy optimization with format, temporal, and spatial rewards. LongEarth-R1 achieves the best results on all 12 long-sequence tasks while remaining competitive on standard remote sensing benchmarks.

</details>


### [145] [Rules or Character? Scaling Laws for AI Safety Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13345)
*Satoshi Takahashi,Nobuji Kouno,Masaaki Komatsu,Ryuji Hamamoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简化比较静态模型，将AI安全设计参数化为性格塑造（如RLHF）与规则执行（如输出过滤器）之间的资源分配α，并分析了随部署规模T增大时最优平衡的变动方向。研究发现最优α*在多数场景下位于内部解或规则执行边界，且随T增大向性格塑造方向偏移（Δα*=+0.01至+0.21）。关键发现是基线性格脆弱率p^0_frag是主导参数（对α*的影响达0.50），远超尾部风险、过滤器质量或共因故障概率的影响；CVaR与期望损失的最优解在大规模部署时趋于一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI安全系统普遍同时采用性格塑造（训练时的RLHF、宪法AI等）和规则执行（推理时的输出过滤器、安全分类器等），但缺乏关于这两者最优平衡应如何随部署规模扩大而变化的形式化分析。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 构建一个风格化的比较静态模型，将安全设计参数化为α∈[0,1]的资源分配权重，纳入规模依赖的过滤器退化、共因故障和性格脆弱性三个因素。在乘法Pareto损失模型下推导期望损失的闭式解，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟补充尾部风险（CVaR）分析。在乐观、中度和悲观三种场景下求解最优α*并分析其敏感性。

Result: 三种场景下最优α*均为内部解或位于规则执行边界；随部署规模T增大，最优解向性格塑造方向偏移，但偏移幅度取决于场景（从+0.01到+0.21不等）。基线性格脆弱率p^0_frag是主导敏感性参数，对α*的影响幅度（0.50）远超其他参数；CVaR与期望损失在T较大时收敛到一致的最优解。

Conclusion: 安全架构决策对部署规模本身的依赖程度低于对性格塑造在分布偏移下可靠性的依赖。这暗示在部署安全系统时，应更关注性格塑造方法的鲁棒性（即其在分布转移下能否保持良好行为），而非单纯依据部署规模来调整安全资源配置。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety systems combine character shaping (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback [RLHF], Constitutional AI), which modifies behavioral distributions at training time, with rule enforcement (e.g., output filters, safety classifiers), which blocks harmful outputs at inference time, yet little formal analysis exists on how their optimal balance should change as deployment scales increase. We introduce a stylized comparative-statics model that parameterizes safety design as a resource allocation alpha in [0,1] between these two approaches, incorporating scale-dependent filter degradation, common-mode failures, and character fragility -- the risk that shaped behavior degrades or collapses under novel conditions. Under a multiplicative Pareto damage model, we derive closed-form expected harm and supplement it with tail-risk (CVaR) analysis via Monte Carlo simulation. Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [146] [TopoIntent: Compiling Security Intent into Executable, Compliance-Checked Network Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13389)
*Xiaokang Qu,Jianliang Ma,Zao Fan,Tianshu Chu,Tianlong Fan,Linyuan Lü*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoIntent是一个将自然语言安全意图编译为可执行、可合规校验的网络拓扑的系统。它通过模式契约约束生成、密集向量检索参考架构模板、分阶段融合实现意图-模板对齐与安全补全，生成的拓扑经过CIS Controls v8.1.2校验并自动修复结构缺口，最终导出为带内核级iptables ACL的Mininet脚本。在自构建的数据集上，加法修复将拓扑可见CIS满足率从0.78提升至1.00，策略通过率从0.78提升至0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业安全拓扑设计需要将业务意图、监管要求和风险假设转化为区域、边界设备、区域间路径和访问控制策略，但现有NetOps自动化工具通常在设计确定后才介入，缺乏从欠规范的自然语言需求生成结构化安全拓扑的能力。

Method: TopoIntent采用模式契约（schema contract）约束生成过程；通过密集向量搜索从精选模板库中检索参考架构；应用分阶段融合（staged fusion）实现意图与模板的对齐及安全功能补全；生成的拓扑依据CIS Controls v8.1.2在拓扑层可见的防护措施进行校验，未解决情况标记为人工审查；结构缺口通过加法式、保持模式一致性的编辑进行修复；最终拓扑导出为带内核级iptables ACL的Mininet脚本，支持可执行的可达性和允许/拒绝测试。

Result: 由于不存在公开基准，作者从参考安全架构图构建评估集。检索集含22个模板和44个合成意图（覆盖5个场景），保留集含7个模板和14个来自金融与政府场景的意图。在保留集上，加法修复将拓扑可见CIS满足率从0.78提升至1.00（平均少于1.5轮），一次反馈轮将ACL策略通过率从0.78提升至0.88。

Conclusion: TopoIntent证明了从自然语言安全意图自动生成合规且可执行的网络拓扑的可行性，通过模板检索、结构化生成、合规校验和增量修复相结合的方式，在少轮迭代内达到完整的拓扑层合规，为安全拓扑自动化设计提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Enterprise security topology design requires translating business intent, regulatory requirements, and risk assumptions into zones, boundary devices, inter-zone paths, and access-control policies. Existing NetOps automation tools mainly operate after this design is fixed, providing limited support for generating structured security topologies from underspecified natural-language requirements. We present TopoIntent, a system that compiles security intent into executable, compliance-checked network topologies. It uses a schema contract to constrain generation, retrieves reference architectures from a curated template library via dense-vector search, and applies staged fusion for intent-template alignment and security completion. The generated topology is checked against CIS Controls v8.1.2 safeguards visible at the topology layer, while unresolved cases are marked for manual review. Structural gaps are repaired through additive schema-preserving edits. The final topology is exported to Mininet scripts with kernel-level iptables ACLs, enabling executable reachability and allow/deny tests. Because no public benchmark exists for this requirement-to-topology task, we construct an evaluation set from reference security architecture diagrams. The retrieval set contains 22 templates and 44 synthetic intents across five scenarios, while the held-out set contains 7 templates and 14 intents from finance and government scenarios excluded from retrieval. On the held-out set, additive repair improves topology-visible CIS satisfaction from 0.78 to 1.00 in fewer than 1.5 rounds on average, and one feedback round raises the post-ACL policy pass rate from 0.78 to 0.88.

</details>


### [147] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（Transformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation）模型，将学生每学期的课程视为并发序列而非简单顺序序列，联合预测学生即将修读的课程及其对应成绩。基于十年院校数据的实验表明，联合预测课程与成绩可将平均绝对误差降低近50%，优于LSTM序列模型和GNN方法，并可自然融入学生属性数据，适用于高等教育机构的早期预警系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习分析中的预测模型将学生学业历史视为简单的时间序列，忽略了同一学期内多门课程并修的特性，导致对课业负担重或选课密集的学生预测不准确。这一建模简化是预测性能受限的关键原因。

Method: TRACE模型以Transformer为基础架构，按学期对课程进行编码以捕获课程并发效应；使用结合课程集合预测与成绩预测的新型损失函数，在预测未来学期成绩的同时预测该学期学生将修读的课程集；可灵活融入学生属性特征，且支持通过重训练与重新校准迁移到新院校。

Result: 在十年院校数据上，联合预测模型相比仅预测成绩的相同架构将平均绝对误差降低近50%，并优于传统LSTM序列模型和基于图神经网络的方案。

Conclusion: 联合预测课程与成绩是提升学业成绩预测性能的关键技术；现代神经架构能够构建可解释的预测模型，并可通过重训练与重校准适应新机构，有望集成到高校早期检测与干预系统中。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [148] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ParliamentRAG，一个针对意大利众议院会议记录的多视角检索增强生成系统。其核心创新是主题相关的发言人权威度模型，将发言人职业、教育背景和过往干预等可解释特征与当前查询结合，以估计权威度。系统同时解决三大风险：高频发言人主导、无法按主题专长加权发言人、政治敏感文本的引用错误归属。在与 Google NotebookLM 的对比评测中，ParliamentRAG 在跨政治团体覆盖率、引文忠实性及专家对来源相关维度的偏好上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 议会会议记录是民主审议的主要记录，但其体量庞大且碎片化，使公民、记者和研究者难以进行多视角访问。现有的 RAG 应用在议会记录中存在三大特有风险：最频繁发言者的主导效应、无法根据主题专长对发言人加权、以及政治敏感文本中的引用错误归属。这些问题需要被联合解决。

Method: 提出 ParliamentRAG 系统，其核心是主题依赖的权威度模型：该模型将每个发言人的权威度作为当前查询的函数进行估计，结合职业、教育背景和过往干预等可解释组件。系统流程为：针对用户查询检索相关讲话片段，识别跨议会团体的主题相关专家，生成综合不同视角的摘要并附支持性引文。评测采用两层级协议，结合自动化指标和六位领域专家的盲法 A/B 人工评估，与 Google NotebookLM 在 15 个政策主题上对比。

Result: ParliamentRAG 在跨政治团体覆盖率上更高（0.97 vs 0.95），引文忠实性达到完美（1.00 vs 0.95），且领域专家在来源相关维度上更偏好该系统；而 NotebookLM 在散文质量相关维度上仍然更强。

Conclusion: ParliamentRAG 通过主题相关的权威度建模有效缓解了议会记录 RAG 的三大风险，在来源多样性和引文可靠性方面显著优于商业基线系统；但散文流畅性等文本生成质量方面仍有改进空间，提示未来可结合两者的优势。

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对7个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上进行了系统性评估，提出了一种基于规则指标的框架，从方案构思、执行和反馈控制三个维度刻画运行内行为，并通过受控对比评估经验在任务内和跨任务间的复用。结果显示当前智能体更像工程优化器而非完全自主的研究者，其最强解决方案主要改编或组合已有技术，真正的创新仍然罕见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注最终分数，既无法揭示进展得失的具体环节，也无法判断累积经验是否改善后续决策。为了更好地理解自主智能体通过长时程实验改进技术制品的能力现状，需要超越最终分数的系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出了一个新的评估框架，包含：(1) 使用基于规则的指标从方案构思（Solution Framing）、执行（Execution）和反馈控制（Feedback Control）三个维度刻画运行内行为；(2) 通过受控对比评估经验在任务内和跨任务间的复用以衡量经验积累的效果。评估对象为7个前沿模型在36个长时程任务上的表现。

Result: 当前智能体更接近于工程优化器而非完全自主的研究者：它们能够构思并实施实用解决方案，但不同运行之间性能波动显著；最强方案主要改编或组合已有技术，真正的方法论创新仍然稀少。详细分析显示，最终相似的结果背后可能存在不同的流程瓶颈，经验复用既可能帮助也可能误导后续决策，此外设计（harness）设计方案也会影响性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究发现为改进模型训练、推理时策略、经验管理和设计（harness）设计提供了具体方向，表明要推动智能体从工程优化器迈向真正自主的研究者，需要在多方面系统性地改进当前模型的能力。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [150] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用小型语言模型（SLMs）在边缘设备上实现认知具身智能体架构（CEAA）中的"思考"和"记忆"核心组件，并基于NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX开发了边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，通过Qwen2.5系列模型验证了SLM驱动原型智能体的路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能虚拟智能体需要在复杂虚拟世界和元宇宙中保持持久性、自适应性和上下文感知能力，但认知能力的实现面临概念和技术双重挑战。现有CEAA框架提供了组件化实现蓝图，但将感知、记忆、推理、规划等认知组件高效部署在边缘环境中的方案仍缺乏系统验证。

Method: 设计并实现了一个边缘虚拟智能体网关系统，选择CEAA框架中"Think"和"Memory"两个核心认知过程中的组件，利用Qwen2.5系列不同规模的小型语言模型作为推理引擎；系统部署在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX边缘平台上；通过模拟实验评估服务请求的路由准确性、记忆读取性能及端到端延迟。

Result: 系列仿真实验表明，SLM驱动的原型智能体系统能够部分实现CEAA的选定认知过程，在路由准确性、记忆读取性能和延迟方面均展现出可行表现，证明小型语言模型可以在边缘设备上支撑具身智能体的认知"大脑"运行。

Conclusion: 本研究验证了小型语言模型与边缘计算相结合的技术路线，为实现沉浸式虚拟世界中的高效、上下文感知的具身智能体认知架构提供了可行方案，并为未来元宇宙中认知能力可边缘化部署的虚拟智能体发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [151] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一AI就绪度等级（AIRL）九级序数标度和RAIL评估方法，将三个现有框架统一为可由自然语言描述直接判定的可决策标度，并通过LLM专家小组分类器实现自动化评估，测试表明该方法一致性好且避免高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术成熟度评估对投资决策、项目管理和政策监测至关重要，但现有就绪度框架异构且难以自动应用：TRL缺乏AI特定门控标准，MLTRL依赖于内部过程产物的访问权限，AI/数据就绪度维度模型使用的量表难以直接比较。

Method: 提出两个贡献：(1) AIRL统一框架——九级序数标度，基于环境证据阶梯构建，辅以维度上限（涵盖规格、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、专家知识和算法成熟度）、泛化锚定规则和显式指派纪律，使就绪度可从工作自然语言描述单独判定；(2) RAIL分类器——专家小组方法，由一个证据代理和六个独立维度代理（每个均为窄范围的LLM）做出裁决，由确定性最小规则聚合，首席专家在不对称权威下审查，可确认或降低小组建议但绝不超上限提升。测试于多项研究工作的分析。

Result: 该方法在多项研究工作的分析中显示出一致性，并避免了单体LLM分类器的过度估计问题。

Conclusion: AIRL和RAIL为解决AI就绪度评估的异构性和自动化难题提供了一套统一、可判定且可靠的方案，通过多LLM专家分工与不对称审查机制有效提升了评估的稳健性。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [152] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）所采用的组织框架，该框架以学生为中心、基于项目的方式集成教学、科研和大学拓展三大支柱，通过民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织来培养技术能力和横向能力，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响的人工智能应用等代表性项目加以验证，为工程和计算教育中的学术联盟提供了灵活可复制的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育以及加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但缺乏系统化的组织框架来有效整合教学、科研和大学拓展三大支柱，因此需要一个可复制、灵活的框架来指导此类学生组织的运行。

Method: 提出一个结合民主治理、协作学习与动态项目组织的组织框架，采用以学生为中心、基于项目的教学法，并通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库构建及具有社会影响力的人工智能应用等多个代表性项目来展示该框架的实施效果。

Result: 该框架成功支持了多样化的教育、科研和拓展活动在统一组织结构下开展，同时促进了领导力培养、科学产出、社区参与和知识保存，表明该框架具有灵活性和可复制性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为工程与计算教育中整合大学三大支柱提供了一种实用且可复制的模式，可为学术联盟及类似学生组织提供实践指导。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [153] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角系统性地研究了世界模型，提出"因果世界模型"（CWM）的形式化定义，主张有用的世界模型应超越生成能力，捕获实体属性、实体间及实体与环境间的因果交互，并将其与因果表示学习、因果发现、结构因果模型等现有工作联系起来，同时讨论了可辨识性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视为智能体预测、规划和超越训练分布行动的基础，但现有工作多聚焦于生成能力，缺乏对模型内部因果结构的规范性定义。作者认为世界模型应捕获决定系统动态的因果机制，以支撑因果推理和明智决策，因此需要从因果视角重新审视和正式化世界模型。

Method: 作者从多个抽象层次（从感知观察到环境动态的概念结构）研究世界模型，提出了基于其"意图支持的任务"来定义的因果世界模型形式化框架，并将世界建模与因果表示学习、以对象为中心的学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策制定等既有文献连接起来。

Result: 论文提供了CWM的形式化定义，建立了世界模型与因果推理相关领域的联系，并梳理了可辨识性文献，阐明了世界模型的各组件何时能从数据中恢复以及恢复到何种等价程度。

Conclusion: 因果世界模型将世界模型扎根于支持因果推理和知情决策的表征与结构中，为构建能够真正理解和预测环境动态的智能体提供了理论框架基础。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [154] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个开源的多智能体推理与协调框架，用确定性的多智能体编排替代单一大模型提示方式，用于临床推理。它协调角色分工的智能体（提取、推理、答案生成、评估），支持显式上下文传递和可追踪的中间输出，实现分阶段故障归因。框架还引入Decomposer模块，可从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定的智能体提示，无需手动提示工程。支持API和本地CPU部署，完全通过YAML配置，模型无关、可解释，面向无需编程经验的临床专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单一大模型提示方式在临床推理中缺乏可解释性和故障定位能力，且手动提示工程对临床领域专家门槛过高。作者希望提供一种确定性、可解释、模型无关且无需编程技能的临床推理框架。

Method: MARC采用多智能体编排架构，包含提取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色专业化智能体，通过显式上下文传递实现各阶段的中间输出可追溯。此外引入Decomposer模块，从自然语言描述自动生成任务特定的智能体提示，消除手动提示工程需求。框架通过YAML配置驱动，支持API和本地CPU两种部署模式。

Result: 论文摘要中未报告具体的实验对比结果或基准测试数据，仅描述了框架本身的设计和能力。完整的代码已开源在GitHub上。

Conclusion: MARC提供了可解释、可配置、模型无关的多智能体临床推理框架，使非编程背景的临床专家也能够使用和定制AI临床推理流程，并通过阶段级故障归因提高了系统的可调试性。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [155] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlayaWorld的改进版本，在不改变主干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据的前提下，彻底重新设计了条件信号的表示与集成方式，核心原则是让条件信号在潜在表征和时间结构上尽可能与生成内容匹配。具体引入六项修改，包括运动感知的潜在条件、因果编码的空间记忆、像素级时间窗口对齐、硬记忆丢弃、统一的VAE编解码协议以及移除相机AdaLN分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现有AlayaWorld中条件信号与生成内容在潜在表征空间和时间结构上存在不匹配，这种不一致会限制生成质量。因此他们提出一个新原则：条件信号应尽最大可能与生成内容在潜在表征和时间结构上对齐，以此指导条件管道的重新设计。

Method: 保留原有主干架构、分块自回归生成方案和训练数据，对条件表示和集成方式做六项修改：(1) 用运动感知的潜在条件替代静态帧图像条件；(2) 将重新渲染的空间记忆作为连续序列进行因果编码；(3) 在像素空间中对齐时间记忆窗口；(4) 采用硬记忆丢弃（移除记忆token而非置零）；(5) 统一训练和推理阶段的VAE编解码协议；(6) 移除相机AdaLN分支，完全通过重新渲染的空间条件提供视角控制。此外，用流式3D点缓存渲染器替代基于深度扭曲的空间记忆。

Result: 该报告为技术报告形式，主要呈现了新设计方案，未在此摘要中给出具体定量指标或对比实验结果。

Conclusion: 通过上述重新设计，AlayaWorld的条件信号在潜在表征和时间结构上与生成内容保持高度一致，有望改善条件生成的质量与一致性，同时简化模型结构（移除相机分支）。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [156] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuoteBench基准，通过精确最终状态验证区分LLM编码智能体中命令生成错误与执行传输引入的失败。在14个事故衍生任务族的56个一次性任务上交叉生成契约与执行传输（加入一个故意未转义的解析器），实验表明重放相同输出经新增解析器后成功率下降55.4-73.2个百分点，披露边界后六个配置恢复30.4-60.7点。研究发现原始生成在前沿已近饱和，边界适应才是模型区分的关键；匹配得分会掩盖生成损害与补偿的分离（如GPT-5.6-sol的-3.6匹配差距掩盖-64.3损坏和+60.7补偿）；部署配置会重排模型排名。评估命令发布智能体时应报告完整配置而非仅匹配分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体通过会序列化、包装和重新解析模型输出的接口发布Bash命令，仅匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成错误与生成后引入的失败（如解析器、传输层问题）。现有评估将匹配分数视为模型内在属性的做法有缺陷，无法准确定位错误来源。

Method: 构建QuoteBench基准：56个来自14个事故衍生任务族的一次性任务，采用精确最终状态验证；交叉生成契约与执行传输维度，刻意加入一个未转义的解析器；在8个同窗口配置中重放相同回复，测量原始生成、解析器介入后的成功率，以及披露边界后的恢复率。

Result: 重放相同reply经新增解析器后成功率降低55.4-73.2个百分点；披露边界后6个配置恢复30.4-60.7点，2个配置恢复为零或轻微负值。原始生成在前沿模型上接近饱和，边界适应仍区分模型。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距-3.6点掩盖-64.3点生成损害与+60.7点补偿。部署配置在26对可比模型中产生1个明确的排名反转和4个单任务边缘反转。

Conclusion: 评估命令发布智能体时必须报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、工作点和最终状态验证器，而不能将匹配得分当作模型的内在属性。匹配分数掩盖了生成能力与边界适应能力之间的权衡，部署配置的微小差异即可重排模型排名。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [157] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniScientist是一个端到端的全模态AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（如图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图）进行跨学科研究，通过感知层和3个自主智能体（构思、实验、写作）在确定性流水线中运行，完成从原始数据到编译稿件的完整研究流程。在36个真实数据案例中全部成功，平均论文评分6.3，并在所有7个评估维度上超越仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统虽然覆盖了从假设生成到代码执行再到手稿准备的完整研究工作流，但它们通常仅处理文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要，无法访问科学发现所依赖的关键时空、跨通道和程序性证据关系，导致智能体无法获取完整证据支撑。

Method: OmniScientist包含一个感知层和三个自主智能体（构思、实验、写作），在确定性流水线中运作。系统通过哈希代码中的思想、严谨性和声明检查来强制执行新颖性筛选、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性，使原始观测数据在整个研究生命周期中驱动研究问题、实验决策和最终声明。

Result: 在涵盖5个学科家族、4类科学证据、9种模态的36个真实数据案例上，系统在全部36个案例中完成从原始数据到编译稿件的全路径，平均论文评分6.3。与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体配对比较中，直接感知在所有7个评估维度上均胜出，并在85%的头对头评审中获胜。

Conclusion: 生命周期级的感知对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要，OmniScientist证明了直接处理原始多模态证据能够显著提升AI科学家的研究能力，为构建广泛适用的AI科学家提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Weight Certificates for Convex Multi-Objective MPC: Geometric Characterization, $\ell^1$ Construction, and $\ell^2$ Foreclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12520)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Chaitanya Shinde,Miguel Hurtadoand Jerry Lopez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶规则书中词典序优先级约束的加权求和实现问题，证明经典分离启发式(w1>>w2>>...>>wL)问题设置错误，给出了加权和精确复现词典序优化的充要几何条件（单位性能切片的法锥条件），并基于KKT投影系统提出线性规划求解含认证裕度的内点权重；铰链惩罚下可精确实现，平方铰链惩罚下当极限乘子非零时无有限权重可精确实现且违反度按O(1/w)衰减。在nuPlan闭环实验中，11个可校准场景类中9个权重近乎等层分量，法律级事件精度约为匹配启发式权重的两倍，但点态证书表明任意单一权重无法在整段经历中有效，中位寿命仅一个采样间隔，从而促使监控加权求解与选择性级联回退机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规则书按词典序排序规则违反，模型预测控制(MPC)要么通过每tick执行L+1个顺序程序精确执行该排序，要么通过分离启发式w1>>w2>>...>>wL的加权和近似执行。作者揭示该启发式回答了一个错误的问题，需要重新建立加权和与词典序优化之间的精确条件。

Method: 本文从凸优先序规划的几何角度，证明加权和精确复现词典序最优的充要条件是：权重（附加单位性能系数）支撑词典序点处成就映射的上方像，即可行权重构成单位性能切片的向外法锥。在铰链惩罚下该切片可通过缩放KKT系统投影得到一个多面体，线性规划可返回带认证裕度的内点权重；在平方铰链惩罚下，当极限乘子非零时不存在任何有限精确权重。最后在25条规则规则书的nuPlan闭环实验中进行校准与评估。

Result: 在铰链惩罚下可精确复现词典序最优且权重满足可计算的多面体条件；平方铰链惩罚下局部极小值分支的违反度按O(1/w)衰减。nuPlan实验显示11个可校准场景类中9个权重各层分量近乎相等，法律级事件精度约为匹配启发式权重的两倍。然而证书是点态化的：同一权重无法覆盖整个episode的采样时刻，中位寿命仅一个采样间隔，且持久性与活动集稳定性相关；合规模式监控只能检测到部分实测失效。

Conclusion: 分离启发式w1>>w2>>...>>wL并非精确复现词典序优化的正确工具，应当使用基于法锥条件的可计算权重。但由于权重点态有效性限制，实际应用中需要监控式加权求解与选择性级联回退相结合，尽管当前合规监控器检测能力有限，该方案仍是最有前景的方向。

Abstract: Automated-driving rulebooks rank rule violations lexicographically, and model predictive control enforces that ranking either exactly, through $L{+}1$ sequential programs per tick, or approximately, through a weighted sum tuned by the separation heuristic $w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$. We show the heuristic answers the wrong question. For a convex priority-ordered program, a weighted sum reproduces the lexicographic optimum precisely when its weight, augmented by a unit performance coefficient, supports the upper image of the achievement map at the lexicographic point; the admissible weights form the unit-performance slice of an outward normal cone. Under hinge penalties this slice is a polyhedron obtained by projecting a scaled-KKT system, and a linear program returns an interior weight with a certified margin; under squared-hinge penalties no finite weight is exact whenever the limiting multiplier is nonzero, with violation along the local minimizer branch decaying as $O(1/w)$. Calibrated on held-out logs, the resulting weights have near-equal tier components in nine of eleven calibration-eligible scenario classes and roughly double legal-tier event precision against a matched heuristic weight in closed-loop nuPlan experiments on a 25-rule rulebook. The certificate is, however, pointwise: no single weight is valid across the sampled ticks of an episode, the median lifetime is one sampling interval (zero subsequent ticks at the native rate), and persistence tracks active-set stability. These findings motivate monitored weighted solves with selective cascade fallback, although the compliance-pattern monitor detects only a subset of measured lapses.

</details>


### [159] [Optimal Heat Storage Sizing for District Heating Networks to Maximize Electricity Revenue from Combined Heat and Power Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12525)
*Martin Sollich,Maarten Blommaert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数学优化的可扩展自动化方法，用于在区域供热网络（DHN）中优化短期储热容量。该方法通过非线性物理建模同时考虑DHN、热生产者和储热系统，在比利时一个第三代CHP-DHN（30个用户）上验证，最优储热集成可在20年内节省73万欧元（16.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然储热在DHN中的益处已被广泛认可，但由于DHN在热源、生产和消费模式、热网损失及燃料成本等方面的多样性，确定最优储热容量仍然具有挑战性。现有物理基础的储热尺寸优化研究规模有限，且常用简化方法可能无法找到最优储热尺寸。

Method: 采用非线性、基于物理的数学优化方法，对DHN、热生产者和储热系统进行同步建模，以最小化总经济成本。考虑时变的热生产成本、热需求和热损失，通过优化求解确定最优储热容量。

Result: 在比利时第三代CHP-DHN（30个用户）上验证，最优储热集成将20年成本从441万欧元降至368万欧元，节省73万欧元（16.5%）。节省主要来自107万欧元热生产成本的降低（通过将热提取转移到低电价时段），储热投资为32.3万欧元。与简化方法对比，简化方法无法识别最优储热尺寸，体现了该方法的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的整体性、基于物理的优化方法在确保可行设计、准确成本评估和最优储热尺寸确定方面优于常用简化方法，且该方法具有可扩展性，可适用于更大规模及不同类型的DHN系统。

Abstract: Integrating heat storages in district heating networks (DHNs) supports managing dynamic characteristics such as heat-demand variations and changing energy prices, and the intermittency of renewable sources. A common application are DHNs with combined heat and power (CHP) units, where a storage allows shifting heat extraction to periods with favorable electricity prices. Although the benefits of heat storage in DHNs are well established, determining the optimal storage size remains challenging due to the diversity of DHNs in terms of heat sources, production and consumption patterns, network heat losses, and fuel costs. This paper presents a scalable, automated methodology for optimizing short-term heat storage in DHNs using mathematical optimization. The nonlinear, physics-based approach models the DHN, heat producers, and storages simultaneously to minimize total economic cost through optimal storage sizing, explicitly considering time-varying heat-production costs, heat demands, and heat losses. The methodology is demonstrated on a 3rd-generation CHP-DHN in Belgium with 30 consumers, exceeding the scale of previous physics-based storage-sizing studies. For this system, optimal storage integration reduces the 20-year cost by 730 k EUR (16.5%), from 4.41 M EUR to 3.68 M EUR. The reduction results from a 1.07 M EUR decrease in heat-production cost achieved by shifting heat extraction to periods of low electricity prices, while the storage investment amounts to 323 k EUR. A comparison to a commonly used simplified storage sizing method, which fails to identify the optimal storage size, demonstrates the advantage of the proposed holistic, physics-based optimization approach in ensuring feasible designs, accurate cost assessments, and optimal storage sizing.

</details>


### [160] [A Local-Linearly Convergent Algorithm for Nonconvex Equality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12665)
*Frank E. Curtis,Lingjun Guo,Daniel P. Robinson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Gradient-Eigenstep算法的分析：一是证明了当从强二阶稳定点附近初始化、采用足够小的步长和足够大的罚参数时，该算法可获得局部线性收敛速率（此时退化为对Fletcher增广Lagrangian的梯度下降）；二是将该算法用作渐进采样策略的子问题求解器，在处理大样本平均定义的等式约束优化问题时，相比直接求解全样本问题，获得了更好的最坏情况样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸等式约束优化问题在机器学习等大规模应用中广泛存在，Gradient-Eigenstep算法虽能高效地寻找近似二阶稳定点，但缺乏局部收敛速率分析，且在大样本平均场景下其样本效率尚不明确，需要理论扩展以支撑实际应用。

Method: 针对Fletcher增广Lagrangian函数，先从理论上证明Gradient-Eigenstep算法在强二阶稳定点邻域内、适当参数设置下退化为梯度下降并具有局部线性收敛，再将此性质与渐进采样（progressive sampling）策略结合，将其作为子问题求解器以降低总体样本复杂度。

Result: 证明了Gradient-Eigenstep算法在合适初始化与参数条件下具备局部线性收敛速率；将其嵌入渐进采样框架后，在求解由大样本平均定义的等式约束优化问题时，获得了相比直接全样本求解更好的最坏情况样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了Gradient-Eigenstep算法局部收敛性分析的空白，同时展示了其在渐进采样场景下的实用性，为大样本约束优化问题提供了更高效的求解路径。

Abstract: For solving nonconvex equality-constrained optimization problems, a recent Gradient-Eigenstep Algorithm by Goyens et al.~is an iteration-efficient approach, based on minimizing Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian function, for finding an approximate second-order stationary point from an arbitrary starting point. In this paper, the analysis of this algorithm is extended, offering a two-fold contribution. First, it is shown that a local-linear rate of convergence can be obtained by this method if it is initiated sufficiently close to a strong second-order stationary point and employs a sufficiently small step-size parameter and sufficiently large penalty parameter. In this case, the algorithm reduces to a gradient descent algorithm applied to minimize Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian. Second, as a particularly useful application of the first result, it is shown that the Gradient-Eigenstep algorithm can be used as an iteration-efficient subproblem solver in the context of a progressive sampling strategy for solving equality-constrained optimization problems when the objective and constraint functions are defined by large sample averages, ultimately offering an algorithm with an improved worst-case sample complexity when compared to an approach that solves a full-sample problem directly.

</details>


### [161] [Stochastic Mixed-Integer Optimization of Dynamic Electricity Tariffs with Consumer Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12688)
*Ananya Kale,Mohit Apte,Chhaya Gosavi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在收入中性（接近统一电价基准）和限制账单涨幅的约束下，住宅分时电价（day-ahead tariff）设计会损失多少削峰效果。基于5,566户伦敦低碳家庭的半年小时级数据（1.678亿条读数），采用准实验价格响应估计和bootstrap需求情景，求解随机混合整数规划。结果显示：在73个测试日上，分段保护的随机电价平均削峰2.29%（95%CI [2.11,2.48]），收入变化-2.26%，最差分段账单涨幅2.48%；移除分段限价虽可将削峰提升至2.37%，但最差分段账单涨幅升至6.81%。但分段平均保护无法约束家庭尾部风险（p95账单涨幅8.61%，CVaR95为14.76%），采用代表性家庭（尾部感知）变体可将p95降至7.05%而几乎不损失削峰性能（2.28%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前住宅电价必须在需求观测前公布，激进的高电价虽能在仿真中削峰，但可能抬高客户账单。已有文献多聚焦于削峰效果，尚未系统量化"保护性约束"（收入中性与账单涨幅上限）对削峰性能的真实代价，以及分段平均保护与家庭尾部风险之间的差距。本文旨在填补这一量化空白。

Method: 使用伦敦低碳实验（Low Carbon London）5,566户家庭的半小时级数据（1.678亿条经校验读数），以标准电价组为对照进行准实验价格响应估计；通过bootstrap构造需求情景；将电价设计建模为随机混合整数规划（SMP），并采用HiGHS求解器求解；在73个符合条件的留出测试日上评估多种约束方案（分段保护、无分段落、代表性家庭尾部感知变体），沿"保护价格"前沿进行系统对比。

Result: 在全部73个留出测试日上：分段保护随机电价平均削峰2.29%（95%CI [2.11, 2.48]），收入变化-2.26%，最差分段账单涨幅仅2.48%；移除分段限价后削峰仅提升至2.37%，但最差分段账单涨幅升至6.81%，说明在本样本中，大幅平均保护几乎不牺牲削峰性能。然而分段平均上限无法约束家庭分布尾部——家庭95分位账单涨幅达8.61%（CVaR95为14.76%）；改用代表性家庭（尾部感知）变体可将p95降至7.05%，且平均削峰仅从2.29%微降至2.28%，两指标几乎无冲突。

Conclusion: 在不了解批发电价、无法优化利润的前提下，本文证明了"保护性"电价设计约束（收入中性和分段账单上限）并不会显著削弱削峰收益；但分段级平均保护存在"保护幻觉"，需以家庭尾部风险（p95/CVaR）为设计目标。研究提供了完整的"保护价格前沿"（price-of-protection frontier），为监管者与公用事业在电价公平性与系统效率之间的权衡提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Day-ahead residential tariffs must be posted before demand is observed. Aggressive high prices can cut peaks in simulation, but they can also raise customer bills. This paper measures how much peak reduction is lost when a tariff design problem is required to keep revenue near a flat-tariff baseline and to limit bill increases. Using half-hourly data from 5,566 Low Carbon London households (167.8 million validated readings), we estimate quasi-experimental price response against the standard-tariff comparison group, form bootstrap demand scenarios, and solve stochastic mixed-integer programs with HiGHS. On all 73 eligible held-out test days, a segment-protected stochastic tariff reduces simulated peak demand by 2.29% on average (95% day-bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]), with revenue change -2.26% and mean worst-segment bill increase 2.48%. Removing the segment bill cap raises peak reduction only to 2.37%, while the worst segment bill increase rises to 6.81%: in this sample, substantial average protection costs little peak-shaving performance. The same schedules leave a household 95th-percentile bill increase of 8.61% (CVaR95 14.76%), so segment-average caps do not bound household tails. We report the full price-of-protection frontier under consistent household simulation and compare segment protection with a representative-household (tail-aware) variant: the latter cuts household p95 from 8.61% to 7.05% while changing mean peak reduction only from 2.29% to 2.28%. All optimized outcomes are model-based counterfactuals under an opt-in trial; wholesale costs are unavailable, so we do not optimize profit.

</details>


### [162] [An Algebraic Method for Optimizing the State, Control, and Terminal State Weight Matrices for Optimal Feedback Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12702)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim,James D. Turner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用闭式解生成最优反馈控制算法中权重矩阵的优化方法，消除了传统方法中昂贵的数值积分过程。通过闭式代数方程计算Riccati矩阵、状态轨迹、状态转移矩阵和性能指标的偏导数，并用于生成解析梯度以执行优化，最终最小化终端状态值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优反馈控制算法中的权重矩阵（状态惩罚、控制惩罚和终端状态惩罚矩阵）通常通过实验性和迭代性方式选择，这一过程计算成本高昂。传统优化过程需要多次数值积分，计算开销大，因此需要一种更高效的方法。

Method: 利用时变Riccati矩阵、状态轨迹、状态转移矩阵和最优性能指标的闭式解替代数值积分；使用闭式代数方程计算所有偏导数，生成解析梯度用于优化步骤；优化目标为最小化反馈控制问题中的终端状态值。

Result: 通过数值示例验证了所提优化算法的有效性。计算过程避免了数值积分，显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于闭式解的权重矩阵优化方法能够高效生成最优反馈控制算法所需参数，预期可广泛应用于科学与工程中的控制理论应用。

Abstract: The necessary conditions for formulating optimal feedback control algorithms have been known for many years. Free parameters exist in the performance index in the form of state and control penalty and terminal state penalty matrices for tuning the performance of the optimally controlled system. The selection process is typically experimental and iterative. To generate the weight matrices for optimal feedback control algorithms, an optimization process is proposed. Typically, the optimization process for the weight matrices requires several numerical integration processes that are computationally expensive; this work overcomes the classical high computational cost by exploiting closed-form solutions for the time-varying Riccati matrix, state trajectories, state transition matrix, and the optimal performance index. Closed-form algebraic equations are used to generate all partial derivative calculations, and no numerical integration is required. The closed-form partial derivatives are used to generate analytic gradients for the optimization steps. The optimization strategy seeks to minimize the terminal state values for the feedback control problem. A numerical example is presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed optimization algorithm. The resulting computational procedures are expected to be broadly useful for control theory applications in science and engineering.

</details>


### [163] [Efficient Hessian-Free Methods for Multi-Objective Bilevel Optimization with Nonconvex Lower Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12704)
*Yicong Jiang,Feihu Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MOMEHA算法，利用Moreau包络将多目标双层优化转化为带包络约束的单层优化，解决了下层问题非凸的情况，并进一步提出基于动量的MB-MOMEHA变体处理随机场景，在少样本元学习和神经架构搜索实验中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标双层优化在AI领域（如自动化学习、多任务元学习）有广泛应用，但现有方法依赖于下层问题（强）凸的假设，而实际多目标双层学习问题通常是非凸的，下层问题尤其如此。现有方法无法处理这一现实场景，存在方法空白。

Method: 利用Moreau包络将原始多目标双层优化问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化问题；引入光滑加权Tchebycheff标量化方法，使算法在多目标设置下保持单循环（single-loop）且免Hessian（Hessian-free）的计算优势；并提出基于动量的MB-MOMEHA变体以求解随机多目标双层学习问题。

Result: 在确定性和随机设置下均提供了算法的收敛性保证；在少样本元学习和神经架构搜索实验上，该方法在Pareto前沿上优于现有方法，验证了其有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MOMEHA及其动量变体有效填补了非凸下层多目标双层优化方法的空白，在保持单循环免Hessian计算效率的同时获得了收敛保证和优异的实证表现。

Abstract: Multi-objective bilevel optimization has wide applications in the AI area such as automated learning and multi-task meta-learning. Although recently some works have been begun to study the multi-objective bilevel optimization, the proposed methods rely on the (strongly) convex lower level problems. In fact, these multi-objective bilevel learning problems are generally nonconvex, and particularly their lower level problems are nonconvex. To fill this gap, we propose a class of Multi-Objective Moreau Envelope based Hessian-free Algorithms (MOMEHA) to solve the multi-objective bilevel learning problems with nonconvex lower level. Specifically, our method uses the Moreau envelope to convert the original problem into a multi-objective single-level optimization with an envelope constraint. In particular, our method retains computational advantages of being single-loop and Hessian-free in the multi-objective setting by incorporating a smooth weighted Tchebycheff scalarization. Furthermore, we propose a momentum-based variant of MOMEHA (i.e., MB-MOMEHA) method to solve the stochastic multi-objective bilevel learning problems. In theory, we provide the convergence properties of our algorithms under both deterministic and stochastic setting. Some experiments on few-shot meta-learning and neural architecture search demonstrate that our methods outperform the existing approaches in Pareto front, validating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [164] [Shape Design for Degenerate Hyperbolic Equation with Degenerate Boundary and Its Application to Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12754)
*Dong-Hui Yang,Yuanzhi Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类退化双曲型方程的可观性与可控性，其控制区域与退化集相交。与以往控制区域远离退化点的研究不同，本文考虑了控制区域到达退化部分的情形。通过引入基于形状设计的近似方法，将退化方程用一族一致双曲方程逼近，建立近似方程的可观性不等式（常数与近似参数无关），再通过退化极限得到原始退化方程的可观性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于退化双曲方程可控性/可观性的研究主要处理控制区域与退化集分离的情形，而控制区域与退化集相交的情况尚未被充分研究，且退化性本身给分析带来本质困难。

Method: 提出了一种基于形状设计的近似方法：将退化方程近似为一族一致双曲方程。证明过程依赖于：相关算子的谱逼近、弱解的精确估计以及乘子方法。首先建立近似方程的可观性不等式（常数不依赖于近似参数），然后通过退化极限过程获得原始退化方程的可观性不等式。

Result: 建立了退化双曲方程在控制区域与退化集相交情形下的可观性不等式，并由此可推导相应的可控性结果。

Conclusion: 本文填补了控制区域与退化集相交情形下退化双曲方程可控性/可观性研究的空白，所提出的形状设计近似方法为处理退化问题提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the observability and controllability of a class of degenerate hyperbolic equations with a control region intersecting the degenerate set. Unlike the existing results that mainly deal with control regions separated from the degeneracy, we consider the case where the control region reaches the degenerate part. To handle the difficulty caused by the degeneracy, we introduce a shape-design-based approximation method based on shape design by approximating the degenerate equation with a family of uniformly hyperbolic equations. The proof relies on the spectral approximation of the associated operators, precise estimates for weak solutions, and the multiplier method. We first establish observability inequalities for the approximating equations with constants independent of the approximation parameter. Then, by passing to the degenerate limit, we obtain the observability inequality for the original degenerate equation.

</details>


### [165] [Difference-of-Convex Regularization for Graph Learning by Differentiable Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12757)
*Liping Tao,Chee Wei Tan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种差凸正则化（DCR）图学习框架，通过对偶表示近似图拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用，无需直接求逆即可高效解决拉普拉斯正则化非负最小二乘（LR-NNLS）问题，并提供了稳定性和唯一不动点的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉普拉斯正则化最小化是信号处理与机器学习中的基础问题，但图拉普拉斯伪逆矩阵是稠密且通常病态的，导致在大规模场景下直接计算不可行。同时，伪逆学习比拉普拉斯学习更具挑战性。现有方法受限于此，亟需一种无需显式求逆即可近似伪逆谱作用的高效方法。

Method: 在给定图拉普拉斯的设定下，提出差凸正则化（DCR）图学习框架。通过正则化最大似然估计（MLE）近似拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用；利用对偶表示将LR-NNLS问题重构，将伪逆学习与实例特定推断解耦；通过可微分的对偶引导学习方案实现高效的原始解重构，并建立算法稳定性及唯一不动点存在的理论保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，DCR框架在性能上优于凸求解器和图滤波基线方法，并且在多种图拓扑下均表现出稳健的性能。

Conclusion: DCR框架通过避免直接矩阵求逆，成功实现了拉普拉斯伪逆谱作用的高效近似，在理论上具备稳定性与唯一解保证，在实践中优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Laplacian-regularized minimization is fundamental in signal processing and machine learning, but is limited by the dense and ill-conditioned nature of the graph Laplacian pseudoinverse. While the Laplacian itself is sparse, its pseudoinverse is dense and often ill-conditioned, rendering direct computation impractical at scale. Moreover, pseudoinverse learning is more challenging than Laplacian learning. To address this challenge, this paper considers the setting where the graph Laplacian is given and proposes a Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR) graph learning framework that approximates the spectral action of the Laplacian pseudoinverse without direct inversion via regularized Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE). By reformulating Laplacian-Regularized Nonnegative Least Squares (LR-NNLS) through a dual representation, DCR decouples pseudoinverse learning from instance-specific inference and enables efficient primal solution reconstruction via a differentiable dual-guided learning scheme. We establish theoretical guarantees on stability and the existence of a unique fixed point for DCR algorithm. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved performance over convex solvers and graph filtering baselines and robust performance across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [166] [A penalty-type method for relaxed inverse optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12769)
*Floriane Mefo Kue,Patrick Mehlitz,Thorsten Raasch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种罚函数型方法，用于求解逆最优控制中的一类双层优化问题，通过惩罚松弛值函数约束并解耦上下层决策变量的更新来计算驻点，无需线搜索且每轮仅评估一次下层问题，惩罚参数也无需趋于无穷，在合理假设下获得了子序列收敛结果，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优控制问题可以建模为一类双层优化问题，但这类问题通常难以直接数值求解。现有的方法往往需要线搜索、多次下层问题求解或令惩罚参数趋于无穷，计算代价高昂且收敛性难以保证。因此，需要设计一种更高效、更实用的数值方法。

Method: 提出一种罚函数型方法，其核心策略包括：(1) 将松弛值函数约束作为惩罚项引入目标函数；(2) 上下层决策变量的更新相互解耦；(3) 仅当可行性度量未获得充分改进时才增大惩罚参数；(4) 不进行线搜索，每轮迭代仅需评估一次下层问题。目标是通过求解一系列代用品问题的驻点，逼近原问题松弛值函数重构的驻点。

Result: 在合理假设下，该方法获得了子序列收敛性结果。数值实验中，松弛参数同时被驱动至零，实验结果展示了该方法的有效性和实际求解能力。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了传统方法中线搜索和惩罚参数趋于无穷的要求，在显著降低计算成本的同时仍保持子序列收敛性，为逆最优控制中双层优化问题的数值求解提供了一种高效可行的方案。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the introduction and analysis
  of a penalty-type method for the numerical treatment of
  a class of bilevel optimization problems arising from inverse optimal control.
  The algorithm is designed to compute stationary points of the associated relaxed value function reformulation.
  This is achieved by determining a sequence of stationary points associated with a sequence of surrogate problems
  where the relaxed value function constraint is penalized,
  where the updates of upper- and lower-level decision variables are decoupled,
  and where the penalty parameter is enlarged only in those iterations
  which do not come along with a sufficient improvement of some feasibility measure.
  The resulting method does not comprise any linesearch, the lower-level problem has to be evaluated just once per iteration,
  and the penalty parameter does not need to be driven to infinity.
  Nevertheless, subsequential convergence results are obtained under reasonable assumptions.
  Numerical experiments, where the relaxation parameter is also driven to zero, visualize effectiveness of the approach.

</details>


### [167] [Mean Field Stackelberg Game for Production and Carbon Emission Reduction with State Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12775)
*Lijun Bo,Zhen Liu,Jingfei Wang,Jiacheng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两层Stackelberg博弈框架，结合均场交互与共同噪声，在状态反射的排放动态下建模全球气候治理中的中央监管者与异质竞争地区之间的层级决策问题，并证明了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球变暖由人为碳排放驱动，具有跨边界污染特征和不可逆破坏性，对人类构成生存威胁，需要层级化的决策框架来建模和解决气候治理中的策略互动问题。

Method: 构建包含控制变量的均场交互和共同噪声的两层Stackelberg博弈；中央监管者（领导者）通过调整产品价格引导n个异质竞争地区（追随者），并通过反射机制（局部时间过程）强制执行硬性排放上限来模拟紧急减排；最终证明近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性。

Result: 建立了该博弈框架下近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性定理，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行了灵敏度分析。

Conclusion: 该框架为全球气候治理中的层级决策提供了严格的博弈论建模工具，所建立的均衡存在性结果和模拟分析为政策制定者优化碳定价与排放监管策略提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Global warming, driven by anthropogenic carbon emissions with transboundary pollution characteristics and irreversible damage, poses an existential threat to human society. This paper develops a novel two-level Stackelberg game with mean field interaction of controls and common noise which integrates hierarchical decision-making under a state-reflected emission dynamics. A central regulator (leader) adjusts product prices to guide $n$ heterogeneous competing regions (followers) while enforcing a hard emission cap via a reflection mechanism that models emergency reductions through a local time process. We establish the existence of an approximate Stackelberg equilibrium and perform sensitivity analysis via Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>


### [168] [Feedback Diagonalization and Stabilization for Discrete Linear Ensemble Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12813)
*Jonathan M. Bosnich,Xudong Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究可数无限多个线性标量控制系统组成的离散线性集合系统（ensemble system）的反馈镇定问题。针对系统极点位于开左半平面时闭环系统仍不稳定的情况，给出了存在静态线性反馈控制律使闭环系统极点位于闭左半平面且生成元可对角化的充要条件，即序列(|b_n|)和(a_n/|b_n|)的指数衰减是必要的，且在特定极点假设下是充分的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与有限维线性系统不同，无限维线性系统即使极点位于开左半平面也不能保证稳定性。只有当系统的极点位于闭左半平面且其无穷小生成元可对角化时，稳定性才能得到保证。因此需要研究离散线性集合系统是否存在共同反馈控制律，使得闭环系统满足上述两个条件。

Method: 针对由(a_n,b_n)表示的可数无限多个线性标量控制系统，其中所有系统由一个公共标量控制输入驱动。分析静态线性反馈控制的存在性条件，将问题转化为研究闭环系统的极点位置与无穷小生成元的可对角化性质，并推导出关于序列指数衰减的必要和充分条件。

Result: 给出了存在静态线性反馈控制律使闭环集合系统满足稳定性条件的充要条件：序列(|b_n|)和(a_n/|b_n|)的指数衰减是必要的，并且在关于期望极点的一定假设下，该条件是充分的。

Conclusion: 对于离散线性集合系统，反馈可镇定的充要条件涉及系数序列的指数衰减性质，这为无限维集合系统的控制器设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We consider a countably infinite collection of linear, scalar control systems, where each system is represented by the pair $(a_n,b_n)$ with $a_n>0$ for $n\in \mathbb{N}$, and all systems are forced by a common scalar control input. We refer to this collection of systems as a discrete linear ensemble system. The ensemble system is feedback stabilizable if there exists a common feedback control input that asymptotically stabilizes every system simultaneously. Unlike finite-dimensional linear systems, an infinite-dimensional linear system is not guaranteed to be stable if its poles lie in the open left half-plane. Stability is guaranteed, however, if (i) its poles are contained in the closed left half-plane and (ii) its infinitesimal generator is diagonalizable. In this paper, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a static, linear feedback control law that ensures the closed-loop ensemble system satisfies (i) and (ii). In particular, we show that the exponential decay of the sequences $(|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ and $(a_n/|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ is necessary and, under an assumption on the desired poles, sufficient.

</details>


### [169] [A Lebeau--Robbiano approach to the controllability of the Boussinesq system with a reduced number of controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12815)
*Víctor Hernández-Santamaría*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少控制数量的条件下的局部零能控性，控制仅作用于温度方程和N-2个速度分量。基于Lebeau-Robbiano谱方法，通过混合可观性估计克服耦合系统缺乏共同谱分解的困难，并给出小时间控制代价的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boussinesq系统是描述自然对流的重要数学模型，其能控性问题具有重要的理论与应用价值。在减少控制数量的约束下证明其能控性是一个具有挑战性的开放问题。主要困难在于系统由Stokes算子（速度方程）和Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子（温度方程）分别控制，二者的谱分解不兼容，传统分量级谱方法无法直接应用于耦合系统。

Method: 采用Lebeau-Robbiano谱方法框架。核心创新是：(1) 利用线性化系统的级联结构，提出混合可观性估计——仅对Stokes分量进行谱局部化，而热量分量不做频率限制；(2) 结合Stokes高频耗散性质，对线性化系统执行Lebeau-Robbiano迭代；(3) 通过时间迭代论证将线性能控性估计转移到非线性系统。

Result: 证明了二维和三维Boussinesq系统在减少控制数量条件下的局部零能控性，控制数量从标准的N个减少到N-1个（温度方程1个+N-2个速度分量）。控制代价具有小时间界exp(C/T)（即C exp(C/T)）形式，恢复了抛物型系统预期的代价增长规律。

Conclusion: 该结果在减少控制数量的前提下保持了系统的可控制性，并且控制代价的指数形式与标准抛物型系统的预期一致，说明减少控制数量不损害控制的最优时间尺度。所提出的混合可观性估计方法为处理由不同算子控制的耦合系统提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the local null controllability of the Boussinesq system in dimensions two and three with a reduced number of localized controls. More precisely, the controls act on the temperature equation and on only $N-2$ components of the velocity equation. Our proof is based on a spectral approach in the spirit of the Lebeau--Robbiano method.
  The main difficulty comes from the coupled structure of the system. The velocity and temperature equations are governed by the Stokes operator and the Dirichlet Laplacian, respectively. Their natural spectral decompositions are different, and there is no common spectral localization naturally adapted to the coupled system. Therefore, the usual componentwise spectral argument cannot be applied directly. We show that the cascade structure of the linearized system makes it possible to overcome this difficulty. The main ingredient is a mixed observability estimate in which only the Stokes component is spectrally localized, while the heat component is treated without any frequency restriction. Combining this estimate with the dissipation of the high Stokes frequencies allows us to carry out a Lebeau--Robbiano iteration for the linearized system. The resulting linear controllability estimate is transferred to the nonlinear Boussinesq system through a time-iteration argument. As an important consequence, the controls have a small-time cost bounded by $C\exp(C/T)$, which recovers the expected parabolic order while preserving the reduced number of controls.

</details>


### [170] [Distribution Steering via Sliced Optimal Transport Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12828)
*Kaito Ito,Anqi Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于切片最优传输（Sliced Optimal Transport, SOT）的有限时域控制框架，用于将动力系统的状态分布从给定的初始分布驱动到终端分布。通过在每个采样时刻利用投影最优传输映射定义方向性终端条件，并采用最小能量实现得到随机化单方向控制器，再对投影方向求平均获得确定性切片反馈。作者证明了该框架在单积分器动力学下能使切片Wasserstein距离非增，对高斯端点分布保持高斯性和仿射性，并给出了线性能量衰减的理论保证及控制能量的显式刻画，同时进一步将构造推广到线性可控系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布控制（distribution steering）旨在设计反馈律将系统状态分布从初始分布驱动到目标分布。虽然最优传输提供自然的几何方法，但其实现通常需要在全状态空间中构建传输映射或耦合，计算代价高昂。切片最优传输通过一维投影避免了全维构造，但其投影映射只给出方向性位移，不能直接构成可实现的反馈律，因此需要开发一种能将这些投影信息转化为实际可执行反馈控制的框架。

Method: 该方法在有限时域内，于每个采样时刻将目标分布向随机方向进行一维投影，通过切片最优传输得到投影空间中的最优传输映射，将其作为方向性终端条件；然后通过最小能量控制律实现该终端条件，得到随机单方向控制器；对所有投影方向取平均，得到确定性切片反馈律。作者进一步对单积分器系统建立了Wasserstein距离非增性质，对高斯分布给出仿射性和均协方差精确跟踪的证明，并推导了依赖分布（law-dependent）的增益使切片Wasserstein距离线性衰减，同时给出控制能量的显式上界。最后通过可达性归一化坐标和局部可控性Gramian将方法推广到一般线性系统。

Result: 对于单积分器动力学，平均切片反馈使切片Wasserstein距离非增；对于高斯端点分布，控制器是仿射的、保持高斯性并能将均值和协方差精确驱动到终端值。识别出依赖分布的增益可实现切片Wasserstein距离的线性衰减，并显式刻画了控制能量。还证明了当采样周期趋于零时随机控制器收敛到平均切片流。对线性系统，均匀完全驱动系统可通过可达性归一化坐标瞬时实现切片速度，一般可控系统则利用局部可控性Gramian实现精确有限步完成。

Conclusion: 该工作将切片最优传输与反馈控制有效结合，建立了从投影最优传输映射到可实现反馈律的完整桥梁，为分布驱动控制提供了具有理论保证的高效算法框架，在机器人运动规划、群体控制等分布控制任务中具有广泛应用潜力。

Abstract: Distribution steering seeks feedback laws that drive the state law of a dynamical system between prescribed initial and terminal distributions. Optimal transport provides a natural geometric approach, but its implementation generally requires a transport map or coupling in the full state space. Sliced optimal transport avoids this full-dimensional construction through one-dimensional projections. Yet, the resulting projected maps specify only directional displacements and do not by themselves prescribe a realizable feedback law. To this end, we develop a finite-horizon control framework based on sliced optimal transport. At each sampling instant, a projected optimal transport map defines a directional terminal condition, whose minimum-energy realization yields a randomized single-direction controller. Averaging over projection directions gives a deterministic sliced feedback. For the single-integrator dynamics, the averaged feedback makes the sliced Wasserstein distance to the target non-increasing. For Gaussian endpoint laws, it is affine, preserves Gaussianity, and steers the mean and covariance to their prescribed terminal values. We further identify a law-dependent gain that yields linear decay of the sliced Wasserstein distance together with an explicit characterization of the control energy. We also prove that the randomized controller converges to the averaged sliced flow as the sampling period vanishes. Finally, we extend the construction to linear dynamical systems. Reachability-normalized coordinates allow instantaneous realization of the sliced velocity for uniformly fully actuated systems, while local controllability Gramians provide exact finite-step realization for general controllable systems. Numerical examples illustrate the resulting distributional flows.

</details>


### [171] [Distance-Based Formation Control with Prescribed Performance for Higher-Order Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12896)
*Janina Schaa,Maria Charitidou,Dimos Dimarogonas,Thomas Berger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有预定瞬态性能的分布式距离编队控制问题，针对相对阶大于1的未知非线性多智能体系统。通过引入虚拟领导者并设计分布式无模型控制律，在树图或最小刚性图通信拓扑下实现编队跟踪与质心跟踪，保证编队误差和跟踪误差满足预设的漏斗约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式距离编队控制大多针对相对阶为一阶或二阶的系统，且控制律通常依赖于系统模型或相对速度信息。对于具有任意相对阶未知非线性动力学的多智能体系统，如何实现编队控制并保证瞬态性能（如收敛速度和超调量）仍是一个开放问题。这推动了本文对更高相对阶系统分布式无模型编队控制方法的研究。

Method: 本文提出一种系统性框架：(1) 引入虚拟领导者，其轨迹被指定子集智能体以预定瞬态行为跟踪；(2) 将通信拓扑划分为树图或最小且无穷小刚性图两类分别处理；(3) 在刚性图情形下额外考虑质心跟踪目标；(4) 设计分布式、无模型（不依赖系统动力学模型）的控制律，利用漏斗控制（funnel control）思想将编队和跟踪误差约束在预设的漏斗函数内，而不需要误差导数的信息。

Result: 在树图和最小刚性图两种通信结构下，所提出的分布式无模型控制律均能保证编队误差和跟踪误差严格满足预设的漏斗约束（即瞬态和稳态性能均被保证），并确保闭环所有信号有界。数值仿真验证了所提控制律的有效性，包括相对阶大于1的未知非线性系统场景。

Conclusion: 该工作将分布式距离编队控制从低相对阶/模型已知系统推广到相对阶大于1的未知非线性系统，同时通过漏斗控制实现了预定瞬态性能保证，无需系统模型和误差导数信息，具有重要的理论价值和实践意义。

Abstract: We consider distributed distance-based formation control with prescribed transient performance for multi-agent systems modeled by unknown nonlinear dynamics of relative degree greater than one. We introduce a virtual leader whose trajectory is tracked with prescribed transient behavior by a designated subset of agents. The undirected communication graph is either a tree graph or a minimally and infinitesimally rigid graph. In the latter case, we further consider the objective of centroid tracking. In each setting, a distributed and model-free control law is developed, and we establish the satisfaction of the prescribed funnel constraints on the formation and tracking errors, and boundedness of all closed-loop signals. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed control laws.

</details>


### [172] [Optimizing Multi-Stakeholder Fairness in Computed Tomography Appointment Scheduling with Predicted Scan and Reporting Duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13059)
